# FreeCAD Graphite — attribution and licensing

This is an Aether Studio adaptation of FreeCAD artwork, not an official FreeCAD
icon theme. FreeCAD Graphite is separate from Aether Classic and Graphite Ribbon.

- Upstream: https://github.com/FreeCAD/FreeCAD and https://www.freecad.org/
- Artwork reuse guidance: https://github.com/FreeCAD/FreeCAD-documentation/blob/main/wiki/Frequently_asked_questions.md#can-i-reuse-any-part-of-the-freecad-artwork-or-pieces-of-the-website
- Local source snapshot: `FreeCAD-main/`; exact per-file SHA-256 in `pack.json`.
- Modifications by Aether Studio contributors, 2026-09-24: graphite/silver/blue
  palettes, fine outline treatment, removal of editor metadata and unused defs,
  and 32px display size. Silhouettes remain derived from FreeCAD.
- **Copyright and licenses remain with the listed original authors.** The SVGs
  are not relicensed as proprietary Aether artwork. These modifications are
  supplied under the applicable original icon licenses.
- `originals/` contains the complete, unmodified source SVGs including embedded
  metadata. `build.py` supplies the corresponding editable transformation source.
  Generated SVG descriptions retain authors, source path, license references and
  modification notice. Keep this file, source SVGs and license notices with
  redistributed copies of the pack.

FreeCAD's repository license is LGPL 2.1 (`LICENSE-LGPL-2.1.txt`). Some SVGs
carry more specific LGPL or Creative Commons notices. The table preserves every
explicit declaration rather than flattening the pack to one license. Where
metadata contains both LGPL and CC declarations, both are retained. Per-file
metadata takes precedence over this summary. The artwork licenses apply to this
pack; importing it does not change the license declaration of other icon packs.

## Original Aether additions

The 24 masters in `masters/`, listed in `extensions.json`, are original
illustrations by Aether Studio contributors (2026-09-24). They supplement the
FreeCAD-derived theme but are not upstream FreeCAD artwork. Their generated
SVG descriptions identify that distinction. `COVERAGE.md` records 99 existing
FreeCAD equivalents and these 24 original designs for the 123 Aether tool names.
The upstream licensing notices below apply to the attributed source-derived icons.
